{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/manufacturing-failure-reason-codebook-normalization\n\nThis skill should be considered when you need to normalize, standardize, or correct testing engineers' written failure reasons to match the requirements provided in the product codebooks. Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"23196e51-9412-443b-85dd-f39a1051edb5","uuid":"c19240f3-299e-48e6-aae5-63b6088c491b","isSynthetic":true}

segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Segmentation strategy:\n    - Split by common delimiters: ', ' '/ ' ' & ' ' 挂 '\n    - Clean up whitespace and noise\n    - Filter out very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Common delimiters to split on\n    delimiters = [\n        (r',\\s*', ','),          # comma with spaces\n        (r';\\s*', ';'),          # semicolon\n        (r'\\s+/\\s+', '/'),       # slash with spaces\n        (r'\\s+&\\s+', '&'),       # ampersand with spaces\n        (r'，\\s*', '，'),         # Chinese comma\n        (r'；\\s*', '；'),         # Chinese semicolon\n        (r'、\\s*', '、'),         # Chinese enumeration\n        (r'\\s+挂\\s+', '挂'),      # Chinese \"hang\" separator\n        (r'\\s+or\\s+', 'or'),     # English \"or\"\n        (r'\\s+and\\s+', 'and'),   # English \"and\"\n    ]\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    # Find all delimiter positions\n    split_points = []\n    for pattern, delim in delimiters:\n        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end()))\n\n    # Sort and deduplicate\n    split_points = sorted(set(split_points))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    for start, end in split_points:\n        segment = text[current_pos:start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:  # Minimum segment length\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, start))\n        current_pos = end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef compute_text_similarity(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute similarity between span text and codebook text.\n    Uses token overlap, fuzzy matching, and keyword presence.\n    Returns score in [0.0, 1.0]\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    # Exact match (case-insensitive)\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Substring match\n    if span_norm in code_norm or code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.9\n\n    # Token overlap\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n    code_tokens = set(code_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    jaccard = overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    return jaccard\n\n\ndef extract_component_references(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (e.g., U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    # Pattern: letter(s) followed by digits\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef extract_keywords(text: str, keywords_list: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from text\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    text_norm = \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Fail code alignment\n    4. Test item alignment\n    5. Component reference matching\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n    weights = {\n        'text_similarity': 0.50,\n        'station_compatible': 0.20,\n        'fail_code_alignment': 0.15,\n        'test_item_alignment': 0.10,\n        'component_match': 0.05\n    }\n\n    # 1. Text Similarity Evidence\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_matches = extract_keywords(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    keyword_sim = min(1.0, len(keyword_matches) / max(1, len(entry.keywords_examples)))\n\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim * 0.8)\n    score += text_sim * weights['text_similarity']\n\n    if text_sim > 0.7:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.2f}\")\n\n    # 2. Station Compatibility\n    station_compatible = record.station in entry.station_scope\n    if station_compatible:\n        score += weights['station_compatible']\n        evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n    else:\n        # Penalty for station mismatch\n        score -= 0.30\n        evidence.append(f\"station_mismatch({record.station}!={entry.station_scope})\")\n\n    # 3. Fail Code Alignment (if fail_code exists)\n    if record.fail_code and record.fail_code.strip():\n        fail_code_matches = False\n        # Check if fail_code is mentioned in standard_label or keywords\n        for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n            if normalize_text(record.fail_code) in normalize_text(kw):\n                fail_code_matches = True\n                break\n        if fail_code_matches:\n            score += weights['fail_code_alignment'] * 0.5\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code_match\")\n\n    # 4. Test Item Alignment\n    if record.test_item and record.test_item.strip():\n        test_item_norm = normalize_text(record.test_item)\n        label_norm = normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n\n        if test_item_norm in label_norm or test_item_norm in normalize_text(' '.join(entry.keywords_examples)):\n            score += weights['test_item_alignment'] * 0.7\n            evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # 5. Component Reference Matching\n    span_components = extract_component_references(span_text)\n    label_components = extract_component_references(entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_components = set()\n    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_component_references(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        if component_overlap > 0:\n            score += weights['component_match'] * min(1.0, component_overlap / len(span_components))\n            evidence.append(f\"component_match={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n\n    # Ensure score is in [0.0, 1.0]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    return score, evidence\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[CodebookEntry, float]],\n    record: TestRecord,\n    segment_index: int,\n    span_text: str\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply deterministic tie-breaking among near-best candidates.\n    Uses record context to ensure reproducibility.\n    Returns index of selected candidate.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return -1\n\n    # Find the best score\n    best_score = candidates[0][1]\n\n    # Find candidates within small margin (within 2% of best)\n    margin = 0.02\n    near_best = [(i, entry, score) for i, (entry, score) in enumerate(candidates)\n                 if score >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][0]\n\n    # Apply deterministic tie-breaking using hash\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}-{segment_index}-{record.station}-{record.fail_code}-{span_text}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # Select one of the near-best candidates deterministically\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][0]\n\n\ndef process_records(\n    records: List[TestRecord],\n    codebooks: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]\n) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process all records and generate normalized segments.\n    Returns (results_list, unknown_confidences, known_confidences)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    unknown_confidences = []\n    known_confidences = []\n\n    # Build station scope map\n    station_scope_map = defaultdict(set)\n    for product, entries in codebooks.items():\n        for entry in entries:\n            station_scope_map[entry.code] = entry.station_scope\n\n    for record in records:\n        product_codebook = codebooks.get(record.product_id, [])\n\n        # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in product_codebook:\n                score, evidence = score_candidate(\n                    span_text, entry, record, station_scope_map\n                )\n                candidates.append((entry, score, evidence))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if candidates:\n                best_entry, best_score, best_evidence = candidates[0]\n\n                # Determine if we should output UNKNOWN\n                # Low score threshold: 0.45\n                unknown_threshold = 0.45\n\n                if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n                    # Weak evidence, output UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    # Confidence for UNKNOWN: lower range, scaled by best_score\n                    confidence = max(0.15, best_score * 0.35)\n                    unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"weak_match(score={best_score:.4f}), evidence={best_evidence[:2]}\"\n                else:\n                    # Good match\n                    pred_code = best_entry.code\n                    pred_label = best_entry.standard_label\n                    # Confidence for known: higher range, scaled by score\n                    confidence = 0.70 + (best_score - unknown_threshold) * 0.30 / (1.0 - unknown_threshold)\n                    confidence = min(0.99, max(0.65, confidence))\n                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"score={best_score:.4f}, evidence={best_evidence[:3]}\"\n            else:\n                # No candidates found\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = 0.10\n                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                rationale = \"no_candidates_in_codebook\"\n\n            normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code,\n                pred_label=pred_label,\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            ))\n\n        # Target: unknown_mean should be significantly lower than known_mean\n    # Unknown: 0.25-0.40, Known: 0.70-0.95\n\n    if unknown_mean > 0.45:\n        # Scale down unknown confidences\n        scale = 0.35 / unknown_mean\n        unknown_conf = [min(0.45, c * scale) for c in unknown_conf]\n\n    if known_mean < 0.65:\n        # Generate output\n    output = {'records': results}\n\n    print(\"\\nWriting output...\")\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    #  codebooks = {}\n    for product in ['P1_POWER', 'P2_CTRL', 'P3_RF']:\n        codebooks[product] = []\n        with open(f'{codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Segmentation strategy:\n    - Split by common delimiters: ', ' '/ ' ' & ' ' 挂 '\n    - Clean up whitespace and noise\n    - Filter out very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Common delimiters to split on\n    delimiters = [\n        (r',\\s*', ','),          # comma with spaces\n        (r';\\s*', ';'),          # semicolon\n        (r'\\s+/\\s+', '/'),       # slash with spaces\n        (r'\\s+&\\s+', '&'),       # ampersand with spaces\n        (r'，\\s*', '，'),         # Chinese comma\n        (r'；\\s*', '；'),         # Chinese semicolon\n        (r'、\\s*', '、'),         # Chinese enumeration\n        (r'\\s+挂\\s+', '挂'),      # Chinese \"hang\" separator\n        (r'\\s+or\\s+', 'or'),     # English \"or\"\n        (r'\\s+and\\s+', 'and'),   # English \"and\"\n    ]\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    # Find all delimiter positions\n    split_points = []\n    for pattern, delim in delimiters:\n        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end()))\n\n    # Sort and deduplicate\n    split_points = sorted(set(split_points))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    for start, end in split_points:\n        segment = text[current_pos:start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:  # Minimum segment length\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, start))\n        current_pos = end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        segments = [(text.strip(), '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef compute_text_similarity(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute similarity between span text and codebook text.\n    Uses token overlap, fuzzy matching, and keyword presence.\n    Returns score in [0.0, 1.0]\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    # Exact match (case-insensitive)\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Substring match\n    if span_norm in code_norm or code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.9\n\n    # Token overlap\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n    code_tokens = set(code_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    jaccard = overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    return jaccard\n\n\ndef extract_component_references(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (e.g., U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    # Pattern: letter(s) followed by digits\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef extract_keywords(text: str, keywords_list: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from text\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    text_norm = normalize_text(text)\n    for kw in keywords_list:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        if kw_norm in text_norm or text_norm.find(kw_norm.split()[0]) >= 0:\n            matches.append(kw)\n    return matches\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord,\n    station_scope_map: Dict[str, Set[str]]\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Fail code alignment\n    4. Test item alignment\n    5. Component reference matching\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n    weights = {\n        'text_similarity': 0.50,\n        'station_compatible': 0.20,\n        'fail_code_alignment': 0.15,\n        'test_item_alignment': 0.10,\n        'component_match': 0.05\n    }\n\n    # 1. Text Similarity Evidence\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_matches = extract_keywords(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    keyword_sim = min(1.0, len(keyword_matches) / max(1, len(entry.keywords_examples)))\n\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim * 0.8)\n    score += text_sim * weights['text_similarity']\n\n    if text_sim > 0.7:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.2f}\")\n\n    # 2. Station Compatibility\n    station_compatible = record.station in entry.station_scope\n    if station_compatible:\n        score += weights['station_compatible']\n        evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n    else:\n        # Penalty for station mismatch\n        score -= 0.30\n        evidence.append(f\"station_mismatch({record.station}!={entry.station_scope})\")\n\n    # 3. Fail Code Alignment (if fail_code exists)\n    if record.fail_code and record.fail_code.strip():\n        fail_code_matches = False\n        # Check if fail_code is mentioned in standard_label or keywords\n        for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n            if normalize_text(record.fail_code) in normalize_text(kw):\n                fail_code_matches = True\n                break\n        if fail_code_matches:\n            score += weights['fail_code_alignment'] * 0.5\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code_match\")\n\n    # 4. Test Item Alignment\n    if record.test_item and record.test_item.strip():\n        test_item_norm = normalize_text(record.test_item)\n        label_norm = normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n\n        if test_item_norm in label_norm or test_item_norm in normalize_text(' '.join(entry.keywords_examples)):\n            score += weights['test_item_alignment'] * 0.7\n            evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # 5. Component Reference Matching\n    span_components = extract_component_references(span_text)\n    label_components = extract_component_references(entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_components = set()\n    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_component_references(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        if component_overlap > 0:\n            score += weights['component_match'] * min(1.0, component_overlap / len(span_components))\n            evidence.append(f\"component_match={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n\n    # Ensure score is in [0.0, 1.0]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    return score, evidence\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[CodebookEntry, float]],\n    record: TestRecord,\n    segment_index: int,\n    span_text: str\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply deterministic tie-breaking among near-best candidates.\n    Uses record context to ensure reproducibility.\n    Returns index of selected candidate.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return -1\n\n    # Find the best score\n    best_score = candidates[0][1]\n\n    # Find candidates within small margin (within 2% of best)\n    margin = 0.02\n    near_best = [(i, entry, score) for i, (entry, score) in enumerate(candidates)\n                 if score >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][0]\n\n    # Apply deterministic tie-breaking using hash\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}-{segment_index}-{record.station}-{record.fail_code}-{span_text}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # Select one of the near-best candidates deterministically\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][0]\n\n\ndef process_records(\n    records: List[TestRecord],\n    codebooks: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]\n) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process all records and generate normalized segments.\n    Returns (results_list, unknown_confidences, known_confidences)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    unknown_confidences = []\n    known_confidences = []\n\n    # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in product_codebook:\n                score, evidence = score_candidate(\n                    span_text, entry, record, station_scope_map\n                )\n                candidates.append((entry, score, evidence))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if candidates:\n                best_entry, best_score, best_evidence = candidates[0]\n\n                # Determine if we should output UNKNOWN\n                # Low score threshold: 0.45\n                unknown_threshold = 0.45\n\n                if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n                    # Weak evidence, output UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    # Confidence for UNKNOWN: lower range, scaled by best_score\n                    confidence = max(0.15, best_score * 0.35)\n                    unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"weak_match(score={best_score:.4f}), evidence={best_evidence[:2]}\"\n                else:\n                    # Good match\n                    pred_code = best_entry.code\n                    pred_label = best_entry.standard_label\n                    # Confidence for known: higher range, scaled by score\n                    confidence = 0.70 + (best_score - unknown_threshold) * 0.30 / (1.0 - unknown_threshold)\n                    confidence = min(0.99, max(0.65, confidence))\n                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"score={best_score:.4f}, evidence={best_evidence[:3]}\"\n            else:\n                # No candidates found\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = 0.10\n                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                rationale = \"no_candidates_in_codebook\"\n\n            normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code,\n                pred_label=pred_label,\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            ))\n\n        # Target: unknown_mean should be significantly lower than known_mean\n    # Unknown: 0.25-0.40, Known: 0.70-0.95\n\n    if unknown_mean > 0.45:\n        # Scale down unknown confidences\n        scale = 0.35 / unknown_mean\n        unknown_conf = [min(0.45, c * scale) for c in unknown_conf]\n\n    if known_mean < 0.65:\n        # Scale up known confidences\n        scale = 0.75 / known_mean\n        known_conf = [min(0.99, c * scale) for c in known_conf]\n\n    return unknown_conf, known_conf\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    codebooks = load_codebooks('/app/data')\n    records = load_test_logs('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(records)} test records\")\n    print(f\"Codebooks: {list(codebooks.keys())}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nProcessing records...\")\n    results, unknown_conf, known_conf = process_records(records, codebooks)\n\n    # 25→    product_id: str\n    26→    code: str\n    27→    standard_label: str\n    28→    category_lv1: str\n    29→    category_lv2: str\n    30→    station_scope: Set[str]\n    31→    keywords_examples: List[str]\n    32→\n    33→    def __post_init__(self):\n    34→        if isinstance(self.station_scope, str):\n    35→            self.station_scope = set(s.strip() for s in self.station_scope.split(';'))\n    36→\n    37→\n    38→@dataclass\n    39→class TestRecord:\n    40→    \"\"\"Represents a test center log record\"\"\"\n    41→    record_id: str\n    42→    site: str\n    43→    product_id: str\n    44→    work_order: str\n    45→    serial_number: str\n    46→    station: str\n    47→    line: str\n    48→    fixture_id: str\n    49→    test_item: str\n    50→    fail_code: str\n    51→    engineer_id: str\n    52→    raw_reason_text: str\n    53→\n    54→\n    55→@dataclass\n    56→class NormalizedSegment:\n    57→    \"\"\"Represents a normalized segment\"\"\"\n    58→    segment_id: str\n    59→    span_text: str\n    60→    pred_code: str\n    61→    pred_label: str\n    62→    confidence: float\n    63→    rationale: str\n    64→\n    65→\n    66→# ============================================================================\n    67→# UTILITY FUNCTIONS\n    68→# ============================================================================\n    69→\n    70→def load_codebooks(codebook_dir: str) -> Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]:\n    71→    \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n    72→    codebooks = {}\n    73→    for product in ['P1_POWER', 'P2_CTRL', 'P3_RF']:\n    74→        codebooks[product] = []\n    75→        with open(f'{codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    76→            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    77→            for row in reader:\n    78→                entry = CodebookEntry(\n    79→                    product_id=row['product_id'],\n    80→                    code=row['code'],\n    81→                    standard_label=row['standard_label'],\n    82→                    category_lv1=row['category_lv1'],\n    83→                    category_lv2=row['category_lv2'],\n    84→                    station_scope=row['station_scope'],\n    85→                    keywords_examples=[kw.strip() for kw in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n    86→                )\n    87→                codebooks[product].append(entry)\n    88→    return codebooks\n    89→\n    90→\n    91→def load_test_logs(csv_path: str) -> List[TestRecord]:\n    92→    \"\"\"Load test center logs\"\"\"\n    93→    records = []\n    94→    with open(csv_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    95→        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    96→        for row in reader:\n    97→            record = TestRecord(\n    98→                record_id=row['record_id'],\n    99→                site=row['site'],\n   100→                product_id=row['product_id'],\n   101→                work_order=row['work_order'],\n   102→                serial_number=row['serial_number'],\n   103→                station=row['station'],\n   104→                line=row['line'],\n   105→                fixture_id=row['fixture_id'],\n   106→                test_item=row['test_item'],\n   107→                fail_code=row['fail_code'],\n   108→                engineer_id=row['engineer_id'],\n   109→                raw_reason_text=row['raw_reason_text']\n   110→            )\n   111→            records.append(record)\n   112→    return records\n   113→\n   114→\n   115→def segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n   116→    \"\"\"\n   117→    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n   118→    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n   119→\n   120→    Strategies:\n   121→    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n   122→    - Clean whitespace\n   123→    - Filter very short fragments\n   124→    \"\"\"\n   125→    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n   126→        return []\n   127→\n   128→    # Split by delimiters (preserve order, prioritize certain delimiters)\n   129→    split_patterns = [\n   130→        r',\\s*',           # comma + space\n   131→        r';\\s*',           # semicolon\n   132→        r'，\\s*',          # Chinese comma\n   133→        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n   134→        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n   135→        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n   136→        r'\\s+and\\s+',      # English and\n   137→        r'\\s+&\\s+',        # ampersand\n   138→        r'\\s+or\\s+',       # English or\n   139→        r'\\s+挂\\s*',       # Chinese \"hang\"\n   140→        r'\\s+已处理[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"already handled\"\n   141→        r'\\s+有问题[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"has issues\"\n   142→    ]\n   143→\n   144→    # Find all split points\n   145→    split_points = []\n   146→    for pattern in split_patterns:\n   147→        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n   148→            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end(), pattern))\n   149→\n   150→    split_points = sorted(split_points)\n   151→\n   152→    # Merge overlapping splits\n   153→    merged_splits = []\n   154→    for start, end, pattern in split_points:\n   155→        if merged_splits and start < merged_splits[-1][1]:\n   156→            # Overlapping, take the longer split\n   157→            merged_splits[-1] = (merged_splits[-1][0], max(merged_splits[-1][1], end))\n   158→        else:\n   159→            merged_splits.append((start, end))\n   160→\n   161→    # Extract segments\n   162→    segments = []\n   163→    current_pos = 0\n   164→\n   165→    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n   166→        segment = text[current_pos:split_start].strip()\n   167→        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n   168→            segments.append((segment, current_pos, split_start))\n   169→        current_pos = split_end\n   170→\n   171→    # Add final segment\n   172→    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n   173→    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n   174→        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n   175→\n   176→    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n   177→    if not segments:\n   178→        clean_text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n   179→        if len(clean_text) >= 2:\n   180→            segments = [(clean_text, 0, len(text))]\n   181→\n   182→    return segments\n   183→\n   184→\n   185→def normalize_text(text: str) -> str:\n   186→    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n   187→    return ' '.join(text.lower().split())\n   188→\n   189→\n   190→def token_overlap_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n   191→    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n   192→    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n   193→    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n   194→\n   195→    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n   196→        return 0.0\n   197→\n   198→    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n   199→    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n   200→    return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n   201→\n   202→\n   203→def substring_match_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n   204→    \"\"\"Check if span is substring of codebook text (or vice versa)\"\"\"\n   205→    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n   206→    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n   207→\n   208→    if span_norm == code_norm:\n   209→        return 1.0\n   210→    if span_norm in code_norm:\n   211→        return 0.85\n   212→    if code_norm in span_norm:\n   213→        return 0.70\n   214→    return 0.0\n   215→\n   216→\n   217→def keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n   218→    \"\"\"Compute keyword matching score\"\"\"\n   219→    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n   220→    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n   221→\n   222→    if not span_tokens:\n   223→        return 0.0\n   224→\n   225→    keyword_matches = 0\n   226→    for kw in keywords:\n   227→        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n   228→        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n   229→        if kw_tokens & span_tokens:\n   230→            keyword_matches += 1\n   231→\n   232→    return min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n   233→\n   234→\n   235→def extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n   236→    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n   237→    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n   238→    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n   239→\n   240→\n   241→def extract_keywords_matched(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n   242→    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from span text\"\"\"\n   243→    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n   244→    matches = []\n   245→    for kw in keywords:\n   246→        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n   247→        if kw_norm in span_norm or any(t in span_norm for t in kw_norm.split()):\n   248→            matches.append(kw)\n   249→    return list(set(matches))\n   250→\n   251→\n   252→def compute_category_match_score(span_text: str, category_lv2: str) -> float:\n   253→    \"\"\"\n   254→    Compute a score based on category match.\n   255→    Some categories are more likely to appear in certain text patterns.\n   256→    \"\"\"\n   257→    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n   258→    category_lower = category_lv2.lower()\n   259→\n   260→    # Map category patterns to keywords\n   261→    category_patterns = {\n   262→        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n   263→        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', '磨损', 'worn', '接触差', '换针'],\n   264→        'COLD_SOLDER': ['虚焊', '浮高', 'cold solder', 'solder', '少锡'],\n   265→        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', '断'],\n   266→        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '连锡', '桥连'],\n   267→        'CURRENT_HIGH': ['静态电流偏高', 'idle current', 'current high'],\n   268→        'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'quiescent'],\n   269→        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', '写入失败', 'program', 'reflash', 'write failed'],\n   270→        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['校验', 'checksum', 'crc', 'verification'],\n   271→        'BOOT_FAIL': ['启动', 'boot', 'startup', 'fail to start'],\n   272→        'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['版本', 'version', 'mismatch', '下错程序'],\n   273→        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['calibration', 'cal', 'data fail'],\n   274→        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', '污染', 'dirty'],\n   275→        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['压痕', 'pressure', 'mark'],\n   276→        'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'electrostatic'],\n   277→        'MISSING': ['missing', '缺少', '缺件', 'not found'],\n   278→        'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', '反装'],\n   279→        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', '连接器', 'wear', '磨损'],\n   280→        'HIGH_RES': ['resistance', '阻值', 'high', '偏高'],\n   281→    }\n   282→\n   283→    for pattern_name, pattern_keywords in category_patterns.items():\n   284→        if category_lower.find(pattern_name.lower()) >= 0 or pattern_name == category_lv2:\n   285→            score = 0.0\n   286→            for keyword in pattern_keywords:\n   287→                if keyword.lower() in span_lower:\n   288→                    score += 0.15\n   289→            return min(1.0, score)\n   290→\n   291→    return 0.0\n   292→\n   293→\n   294→def score_candidate(\n   295→    span_text: str,\n   296→    entry: CodebookEntry,\n   297→    record: TestRecord\n   298→) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n   299→    \"\"\"\n   300→    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n   301→    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n   302→\n   303→    Evidence sources:\n   304→    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n   305→    2. Station compatibility\n   306→    3. Category match\n   307→    4. Component references\n   308→    \"\"\"\n   309→    evidence = []\n   310→    score = 0.0\n   311→\n   312→    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n   313→    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n   314→        # Station mismatch is a hard penalty\n   315→        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n   316→\n   317→    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n   318→\n   319→    # 2. Text similarity: label + keywords\n   320→    label_sim = max(\n   321→        token_overlap_score(span_text, entry.standard_label),\n   322→        substring_match_score(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n   323→    )\n   324→\n   325→    keyword_sim = keyword_match_score(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n   326→    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim)\n   327→\n   328→    score += text_sim * 0.55\n   329→\n   330→    if text_sim > 0.6:\n   331→        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.3f}\")\n   332→\n   333→    # 3. Category-based scoring\n   334→    category_score = compute_category_match_score(span_text, entry.category_lv2)\n   335→    score += category_score * 0.30\n   336→\n   337→    if category_score > 0.3:\n   338→        evidence.append(f\"cat={entry.category_lv2}\")\n   339→\n   340→    # 4. Component reference matching\n   341→    span_components = extract_components(span_text)\n   342→    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n   343→    keyword_components = set()\n   344→    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n   345→        keyword_components.update(extract_components(kw))\n   346→\n   347→    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n   348→\n   349→    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n   350→        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n   351→        comp_score = component_overlap / len(span_components)\n   352→        score += comp_score * 0.15\n   353→        if component_overlap > 0:\n   354→            evidence.append(f\"comp={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n   355→\n   356→    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n   357→    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n   358→\n   359→    return score, evidence\n   360→\n   361→\n   362→def process_records(\n   363→    records: List[TestRecord],\n   364→    codebooks: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]\n   365→) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[float], List[float]]:\n   366→    \"\"\"Process all records and generate normalized segments\"\"\"\n   367→    results = []\n   368→    unknown_confidences = []\n   369→    known_confidences = []\n   370→\n   371→    for record_idx, record in enumerate(records):\n   372→        product_codebook = codebooks.get(record.product_id, [])\n   373→\n   374→        # Segment the raw reason text\n   375→        segments = segment_reason_text(record.raw_reason_text)\n   376→\n   377→        normalized_segments = []\n   378→\n   379→        for seg_idx, (span_text, start_pos, end_pos) in enumerate(segments, 1):\n   380→            segment_id = f\"{record.record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n   381→\n   382→            # Score all candidates\n   383→            candidates = []\n   384→            for entry in product_codebook:\n   385→                score, evidence = score_candidate(span_text, entry, record)\n   386→                if score >= 0:  # Only consider station-compatible entries\n   387→                    candidates.append((entry, score, evidence))\n   388→\n   389→            # Sort by score descending\n   390→            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n   391→\n   392→            if candidates:\n   393→                best_entry, best_score, best_evidence = candidates[0]\n   394→\n   395→                # Threshold for UNKNOWN\n   396→                unknown_threshold = 0.30\n   397→\n   398→                if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n   399→                    # Weak evidence, mark as UNKNOWN\n   400→                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n   401→                    pred_label = \"\"\n   402→                    confidence = 0.20 + (best_score * 0.15)\n   403→                    unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n   404→                    rationale = f\"low_score={best_score:.4f}\"\n   405→                else:\n   406→                    # Good match\n   407→                    pred_code = best_entry.code\n   408→                    pred_label = best_entry.standard_label\n   409→                    # Calibrate confidence based on score\n   410→                    confidence = 0.65 + (best_score * 0.30)\n   411→                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n   412→                    rationale = f\"score={best_score:.4f},ev={','.join(best_evidence[:2])}\"\n   413→            else:\n   414→                # No station-compatible candidates\n   415→                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n   416→                pred_label = \"\"\n   417→                confidence = 0.10\n   418→                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n   419→                rationale = \"no_station_compatible_candidates\"\n   420→\n   421→            normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n   422→                segment_id=segment_id,\n   423→                span_text=span_text,\n   424→                pred_code=pred_code,\n   425→                pred_label=pred_label,\n   426→                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n   427→                rationale=rationale\n   428→            ))\n   429→\n   430→        # Create result record\n   431→        result = {\n   432→            'record_id': record.record_id,\n   433→            'product_id': record.product_id,\n   434→            'station': record.station,\n   435→            'engineer_id': record.engineer_id,\n   436→            'raw_reason_text': record.raw_reason_text,\n   437→            'normalized': [asdict(seg) for seg in normalized_segments]\n   438→        }\n   439→        results.append(result)\n   440→\n   441→        if (record_idx + 1) % 2000 == 0:\n   442→            print(f\"Processed {record_idx + 1} records...\")\n   443→\n   444→    return results, unknown_confidences, known_confidences\n   445→\n   446→\n   447→def calibrate_confidence_distribution(unknown_conf: List[float], known_conf: List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n   448→    \"\"\"\n   449→    Calibrate confidence to ensure proper statistical separation.\n   450→    Target: unknown_mean ≈ 0.30-0.40, known_mean ≈ 0.75-0.85\n   451→    \"\"\"\n   452→    if not unknown_conf:\n   453→        unknown_conf = [0.25]\n   454→    if not known_conf:\n   455→        known_conf = [0.80]\n   456→\n   457→    unknown_mean = sum(unknown_conf) / len(unknown_conf)\n   458→    known_mean = sum(known_conf) / len(known_conf)\n   459→\n   460→    # Calibration scaling\n   461→    target_unknown_mean = 0.35\n   462→    target_known_mean = 0.78\n   463→\n   464→    if unknown_mean > 0:\n   465→        unknown_scale = target_unknown_mean / unknown_mean\n   466→        unknown_conf = [min(0.50, max(0.15, c * unknown_scale)) for c in unknown_conf]\n   467→\n   468→    if known_mean > 0:\n   469→        known_scale = target_known_mean / known_mean\n   470→        known_conf = [min(0.95, max(0.65, c * known_scale)) for c in known_conf]\n   471→\n   472→    return unknown_conf, known_conf\n   473→\n   474→\n   475→def main():\n   476→    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n   477→    print(\"Loading data...\")\n   478→    codebooks = load_codebooks('/app/data')\n   479→    records = load_test_logs('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv')\n   480→\n   481→    print(f\"Loaded {len(records)} test records\")\n   482→\n   483→    print(\"\\nProcessing records...\")\n   484→    results, unknown_conf, known_conf = process_records(records, codebooks)\n   485→\n   486→    # Calibrate confidences\n   487→    print(\"Calibrating confidence scores...\")\n   488→    unknown_conf_calib, known_conf_calib = calibrate_confidence_distribution(unknown_conf, known_conf)\n   489→\n   490→    # Apply calibrated confidences back to results\n   491→    unknown_idx = 0\n   492→    known_idx = 0\n   493→    for result in results:\n   494→        for segment in result['normalized']:\n   495→            if segment['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN':\n   496→                if unknown_idx < len(unknown_conf_calib):\n   497→                    segment['confidence'] = round(unknown_conf_calib[unknown_idx], 4)\n   498→                    unknown_idx += 1\n   499→            else:\n   500→                if known_idx < len(known_conf_calib):\n   501→                    segment['confidence'] = round(known_conf_calib[known_idx], 4)\n   502→                    known_idx += 1\n   503→\n   504→    # Generate output\n   505→    output = {'records': results}\n   506→\n   507→    print(\"Writing output...\")\n   508→    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n   509→        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n   510→\n   511→    # Print statistics\n   512→    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*70)\n   513→    print(\"STATISTICS\")\n   514→    print(\"=\"*70)\n   515→\n   516→    total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in results)\n   517→    unknown_count = sum(1 for r in results for s in r['normalized'] if s['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN')\n   518→    known_count = total_segments - unknown_count\n   519→\n   520→    print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n   521→    print(f\"Known predictions: {known_count} ({100*known_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n   522→    print(f\"Unknown predictions: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n   523→\n   524→    if unknown_conf_calib:\n   525→        unknown_mean = sum(unknown_conf_calib) / len(unknown_conf_calib)\n   526→        unknown_min = min(unknown_conf_calib)\n   527→        unknown_max = max(unknown_conf_calib)\n   528→        unknown_q25 = sorted(unknown_conf_calib)[len(unknown_conf_calib)//4] if len(unknown_conf_calib) > 0 else 0\n   529→        unknown_q75 = sorted(unknown_conf_calib)[3*len(unknown_conf_calib)//4] if len(unknown_conf_calib) > 0 else 0\n   530→        print(f\"\\nUnknown confidence distribution:\")\n   531→        print(f\"  Mean: {unknown_mean:.4f}, Median: {sorted(unknown_conf_calib)[len(unknown_conf_calib)//2]:.4f}\")\n   532→        print(f\"  Min: {unknown_min:.4f}, Q25: {unknown_q25:.4f}, Q75: {unknown_q75:.4f}, Max: {unknown_max:.4f}\")\n   533→\n   534→    if known_conf_calib:\n   535→        known_mean = sum(known_conf_calib) / len(known_conf_calib)\n   536→        known_min = min(known_conf_calib)\n   537→        known_max = max(known_conf_calib)\n   538→        known_q25 = sorted(known_conf_calib)[len(known_conf_calib)//4] if len(known_conf_calib) > 0 else 0\n   539→        known_q75 = sorted(known_conf_calib)[3*len(known_conf_calib)//4] if len(known_conf_calib) > 0 else 0\n   540→        print(f\"\\nKnown confidence distribution:\")\n   541→        print(f\"  Mean: {known_mean:.4f}, Median: {sorted(known_conf_calib)[len(known_conf_calib)//2]:.4f}\")\n   542→        print(f\"  Min: {known_min:.4f}, Q25: {known_q25:.4f}, Q75: {known_q75:.4f}, Max: {known_max:.4f}\")\n   543→\n   544→    print(f\"\\nOutput written to: /app/output/solution.json\")\n   545→    print(\"=\"*70)\n   546→\n   547→\n   548→if __name__ == '__main__':\n   549→ segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Strategies:\n    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n    - Clean whitespace\n    - Filter very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n         # Chinese comma\n        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n        r'\\s+and\\s+', max(merged_splits[-1][1], end))\n        else:\n            merged_splits.append((start, end))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n        segment = text[current_pos:split_start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, split_start))\n        current_pos = split_end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # token_overlap_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n    code_tokens code_tokens)\n    return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef substring_match_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if span is substring of codebook text (or vice versa)\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n    if span_norm in code_norm:\n        return 0.85\n    if code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.70\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute keyword matching score\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keyword_matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n        if kw_tokens & span_tokens:\n            keyword_matches += 1\n\n    return min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef extract_keywords_matched(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from span text\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    matches = []\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        if kw_norm in span_norm or any(t in span_norm for t in kw_norm.split()):\n            matches.append(kw)\n    return list(set(matches))\n\n\ndef compute_category_match_score(span_text: str, category_lv2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a score based on category match.\n    Some categories are more likely to appear in certain text patterns.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n    category_lower = category_lv2.lower()\n\n    # Map category patterns to keywords\n    category_patterns = {\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', '磨损', 'worn', '接触差', 'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['校验', 'checksum', 'crc', 'verification'],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': ['启动', 'boot', 'startup', 'fail to start'],\n        'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['版本', 'version', 'mismatch', '下错程序'],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['calibration', 'cal', 'data fail'],\n        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', '污染', 'dirty'],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['压痕', 'pressure', 'mark'],\n        'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'HIGH_RES': ['resistance', '阻值', 'high', '偏高'],\n    }\n\n    for pattern_name, pattern_keywords in category_patterns.items():\n        if category_lower.find(pattern_name.lower()) >= 0 or return min(1.0, score)\n\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Category match\n    4. Component references\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n        # Station mismatch is a hard penalty\n        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n\n    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n\n    # 2. Text similarity: label + keywords\n    label_sim = max(\n        token_overlap_score(span_text, entry.standard_label),\n        substring_match_score(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    )\n\n    keyword_sim = keyword_match_score(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim)\n\n    score += text_sim * 0.55\n\n    if text_sim > 0.6:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.3f}\")\n\n    # 3. Category-based scoring\n    category_score = compute_category_match_score(span_text, entry.category_lv2)\n    score += category_score * 0.30\n\n    if category_score > 0.3:\n        evidence.append(f\"cat={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 4. entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_components(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        comp_score = component_overlap / len(span_components)\n        score += comp_score * 0.15\n        if component_overlap > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"comp={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n station-compatible entries\n                    candidates.append((entry, score, evidence))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n         # Good match\n                    pred_code = best_entry.code\n                    pred_label = best_entry.standard_label\n                    # Calibrate confidence based on score\n                    confidence = 0.65 + (best_score * 0.30)\n                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = = \"\"\n                confidence = 0.10\n                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                rationale = \"no_station_compatible_candidates\"\n\n 'record_id': record.record_id,\n            'product_id': record.product_id,\n            'station': record.station,\n            'engineer_id': record.engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': print(f\"Processed {record_idx + 1} records...\")\n\n    return results, unknown_confidences, known_confidences\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence_distribution(unknown_conf: List[float], known_conf: List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence to ensure proper statistical separation.\n    Target: unknown_mean ≈ 0.30-0.40, known_mean ≈ 0.75-0.85\n    \"\"\"\n    if not unknown_conf:\n        unknown_conf = [0.25]\n    if not known_conf:\n        known_conf = [0.80]\n\n    unknown_mean = sum(unknown_conf) / len(unknown_conf)\n    known_mean = sum(known_conf) / len(known_conf)\n\n    # Calibration scaling\n    target_unknown_mean = 0.35\n    target_known_mean = 0.78\n\n    if unknown_mean > 0:\n        unknown_scale = target_unknown_mean / unknown_mean\n        unknown_conf = [min(0.50, max(0.15, c * unknown_scale)) for c in unknown_conf]\n\n    if known_mean > 0:\n        known_scale = target_known_mean / known_mean\n        known_conf = [min(0.95, max(0.65, c * known_scale)) for c in known_conf]\n\n    return unknown_conf, known_conf\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    codebooks = site=row['site'],\n                product_id=row['product_id'],\n                work_order=row['work_order'],\n                serial_number=row['serial_number'],\n                station=row['station'],\n                line=row['line'],\n                fixture_id=row['fixture_id'],\n                test_item=row['test_item'],\n                fail_code=row['fail_code'],\n                engineer_id=row['engineer_id'],\n                raw_reason_text=row['raw_reason_text']\n            )\n            records.append(record)\n    return records\n\n\ndef segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Segmentation strategy:\n    - Split by common delimiters: ', ' '/ ' ' & ' ' 挂 '\n    - Clean up whitespace and noise\n    - Filter out very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Common delimiters to split on\n    delimiters = [\n        (r',\\s*', ','),          # comma with spaces\n        (r';\\s*', ';'),          # semicolon\n        (r'\\s+/\\s+', '/'),       # slash with spaces\n        (r'\\s+&\\s+', '&'),       # ampersand with spaces\n        (r'，\\s*', '，'),         # Chinese comma\n        (r'；\\s*', '；'),         # Chinese semicolon\n        (r'、\\s*', '、'),         # Chinese enumeration\n        (r'\\s+挂\\s+', '挂'),      # Chinese \"hang\" separator\n        (r'\\s+or\\s+', 'or'),     # English \"or\"\n        (r'\\s+and\\s+', 'and'),   # English \"and\"\n    ]\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    # Find all delimiter positions\n    split_points = []\n    for pattern, delim in delimiters:\n        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end()))\n\n    # Sort and deduplicate\n    split_points = sorted(set(split_points))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    for start, end in split_points:\n        segment = text[current_pos:start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:  # Minimum segment length\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, start))\n        current_pos = end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        segments = [(text.strip(), 0, len(text))]\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef normalize_text(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison: lowercase, remove extra spaces\"\"\"\n    return ' '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef compute_text_similarity(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute similarity between span text and codebook text.\n    Uses token overlap, fuzzy matching, and keyword presence.\n    Returns score in [0.0, 1.0]\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    # Exact match (case-insensitive)\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Substring match\n    if span_norm in code_norm or code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.9\n\n    # Token overlap\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n    code_tokens = set(code_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    jaccard = overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n    return jaccard\n\n\ndef extract_component_references(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (e.g., U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    # Pattern: letter(s) followed by digits\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef extract_keywords(text: str, keywords_list: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from text\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    text_norm = normalize_text(text)\n    for kw in keywords_list:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        if kw_norm in text_norm or text_norm.find(kw_norm.split()[0]) >= 0:\n            matches.append(kw)\n    return matches\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord,\n    station_scope_map: Dict[str, Set[str]]\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Fail code alignment\n    4. Test item alignment\n    5. Component reference matching\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n    weights = {\n        'text_similarity': 0.50,\n        'station_compatible': 0.20,\n        'fail_code_alignment': 0.15,\n        'test_item_alignment': 0.10,\n        'component_match': 0.05\n    }\n\n    # 1. Text Similarity Evidence\n    label_sim = compute_text_similarity(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_matches = extract_keywords(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    keyword_sim = min(1.0, len(keyword_matches) / max(1, len(entry.keywords_examples)))\n\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim * 0.8)\n    score += text_sim * weights['text_similarity']\n\n    if text_sim > 0.7:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.2f}\")\n\n    # 2. Station Compatibility\n    station_compatible = record.station in entry.station_scope\n    if station_compatible:\n        score += weights['station_compatible']\n        evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n    else:\n        # Penalty for station mismatch\n        score -= 0.30\n        evidence.append(f\"station_mismatch({record.station}!={entry.station_scope})\")\n\n    # 3. Fail Code Alignment (if fail_code exists)\n    if record.fail_code and record.fail_code.strip():\n        fail_code_matches = False\n        # Check if fail_code is mentioned in standard_label or keywords\n        for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n            if normalize_text(record.fail_code) in normalize_text(kw):\n                fail_code_matches = True\n                break\n        if fail_code_matches:\n            score += weights['fail_code_alignment'] * 0.5\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code_match\")\n\n    # 4. Test Item Alignment\n    if record.test_item and record.test_item.strip():\n        test_item_norm = normalize_text(record.test_item)\n        label_norm = normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n\n        if test_item_norm in label_norm or test_item_norm in normalize_text(' '.join(entry.keywords_examples)):\n            score += weights['test_item_alignment'] * 0.7\n            evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # 5. Component Reference Matching\n    span_components = extract_component_references(span_text)\n    label_components = extract_component_references(entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_components = set()\n    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_component_references(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        if component_overlap > 0:\n            score += weights['component_match'] * min(1.0, component_overlap / len(span_components))\n            evidence.append(f\"component_match={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n\n    # Ensure score is in [0.0, 1.0]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    return score, evidence\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[CodebookEntry, float]],\n    record: TestRecord,\n    segment_index: int,\n    span_text: str\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply deterministic tie-breaking among near-best candidates.\n    Uses record context to ensure reproducibility.\n    Returns index of selected candidate.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return -1\n\n    # Find the best score\n    best_score = candidates[0][1]\n\n    # Find candidates within small margin (within 2% of best)\n    margin = 0.02\n    near_best = [(i, entry, score) for i, (entry, score) in enumerate(candidates)\n                 if score >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][0]\n\n    # Apply deterministic tie-breaking using hash\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}-{segment_index}-{record.station}-{record.fail_code}-{span_text}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # Select one of the near-best candidates deterministically\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][0]\n\n\ndef process_records(\n    records: List[TestRecord],\n    codebooks: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]\n) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process all records and generate normalized segments.\n    Returns (results_list, unknown_confidences, known_confidences)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    unknown_confidences = []\n    known_confidences = []\n\n    # Build station scope map\n    station_scope_map = defaultdict(set)\n    for product, entries in codebooks.items():\n        for entry in entries:\n            station_scope_map[entry.code] = entry.station_scope\n\n    for record in records:\n        product_codebook = codebooks.get(record.product_id, [])\n\n        # Segment the raw reason text\n        segments = segment_reason_text(record.raw_reason_text)\n\n        normalized_segments = []\n\n        for seg_idx, (span_text, start_pos, end_pos) in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record.record_id}-S{seg_idx}\"\n\n            # Score all candidates\n            candidates = []\n            for entry in product_codebook:\n                score, evidence = score_candidate(\n                    span_text, entry, record, station_scope_map\n                )\n                candidates.append((entry, score, evidence))\n\n            # Sort by score descending\n            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n            if candidates:\n                best_entry, best_score, best_evidence = candidates[0]\n\n                # Determine if we should output UNKNOWN\n                # Low score threshold: 0.45\n                unknown_threshold = 0.45\n\n                if best_score < unknown_threshold:\n                    # Weak evidence, output UNKNOWN\n                    pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                    pred_label = \"\"\n                    # Confidence for UNKNOWN: lower range, scaled by best_score\n                    confidence = max(0.15, best_score * 0.35)\n                    unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"weak_match(score={best_score:.4f}), evidence={best_evidence[:2]}\"\n                else:\n                    # Good match\n                    pred_code = best_entry.code\n                    pred_label = best_entry.standard_label\n                    # Confidence for known: higher range, scaled by score\n                    confidence = 0.70 + (best_score - unknown_threshold) * 0.30 / (1.0 - unknown_threshold)\n                    confidence = min(0.99, max(0.65, confidence))\n                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = f\"score={best_score:.4f}, evidence={best_evidence[:3]}\"\n            else:\n                # No candidates found\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = 0.10\n                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                rationale = \"no_candidates_in_codebook\"\n\n            normalized_segments.append(NormalizedSegment(\n                segment_id=segment_id,\n                span_text=span_text,\n                pred_code=pred_code,\n                pred_label=pred_label,\n                confidence=round(confidence, 4),\n                rationale=rationale\n            ))\n\n        # Create result record\n        result = {\n            'record_id': record.record_id,\n            'product_id': record.product_id,\n            'station': record.station,\n            'engineer_id': record.engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': record.raw_reason_text,\n            'normalized': [asdict(seg) for seg in normalized_segments]\n        }\n        results.append(result)\n\n    return results, unknown_confidences, known_confidences\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(unknown_conf: List[float], known_conf: List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence values to ensure proper statistical distribution.\n    Returns (calibrated_unknown, calibrated_known)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not unknown_conf:\n        unknown_conf = [0.20]\n    if not known_conf:\n        known_conf = [0.85]\n\n    unknown_mean = sum(unknown_conf) / len(unknown_conf)\n    known_mean = sum(known_conf) / len(known_conf)\n\n    # Target: unknown_mean should be significantly lower than known_mean\n    # Unknown: 0.25-0.40, Known: 0.70-0.95\n\n    if unknown_mean > 0.45:\n        # Scale down unknown confidences\n        scale = 0.35 / unknown_mean\n        unknown_conf = [min(0.45, c * scale) for c in unknown_conf]\n\n    if known_mean < 0.65:\n        # Scale up known confidences\n        scale = 0.75 / known_mean\n        known_conf = [min(0.99, c * scale) for c in known_conf]\n\n    return unknown_conf, known_conf\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    codebooks = load_codebooks('/app/data')\n    records = load_test_logs('/app/data/test_center_logs.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(records)} test records\")\n    print(f\"Codebooks: {list(codebooks.keys())}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nProcessing records...\")\n    results, unknown_conf, known_conf = process_records(records, codebooks)\n\n    # Calibrate confidences\n    print(\"\\nCalibrating confidence scores...\")\n    unknown_conf_calib, known_conf_calib = calibrate_confidence(unknown_conf, known_conf)\n\n    # Apply calibrated confidences back to results\n    unknown_idx = 0\n    known_idx = 0\n    for result in results:\n        for segment in result['normalized']:\n            if segment['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN':\n                if unknown_idx < len(unknown_conf_calib):\n                    segment['confidence'] = round(unknown_conf_calib[unknown_idx], 4)\n                    unknown_idx += 1\n            else:\n                if known_idx < len(known_conf_calib):\n                    segment['confidence'] = round(known_conf_calib[known_idx], 4)\n                    known_idx += 1\n\n    # Generate output\n    output = {'records': results}\n\n    print(\"\\nWriting output...\")\n    with open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # Print statistics\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"STATISTICS\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in results)\n    unknown_count = sum(1 for r in results for s in r['normalized'] if s['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN')\n    known_count = total_segments - unknown_count\n\n    print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n    print(f\"Known predictions: {known_count} ({100*known_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"Unknown predictions: {unknown_count} ({100*unknown_count/total_segments:.1f}%)\")\n\n    if unknown_conf_calib:\n        print(f\"\\nUnknown confidence - Mean: {sum(unknown_conf_calib)/len(unknown_conf_calib):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Min: {min(unknown_conf_calib):.4f}, Max: {max(unknown_conf_calib):.4f}\")\n\n    if known_conf_calib:\n        print(f\"Known confidence - Mean: {sum(known_conf_calib)/len(known_conf_calib):.4f}, \"\n              f\"Min: {min(known_conf_calib):.4f}, Max: {max(known_conf_calib):.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nOutput written to: /app/output/solution.json\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}

segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Strategies:\n    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n    - Clean whitespace\n    - Filter very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Split by delimiters\n    split_patterns = [\n        r',\\s*',           # comma + space\n        r';\\s*',           # semicolon\n        r'，\\s*',          # Chinese comma\n        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n        r'\\s+and\\s+',      # English and\n        r'\\s+&\\s+',        # merged_splits.append((start, end))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n        segment = segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        clean_text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        if len(clean_text) >= 2:\n            segments = [(clean_text, 0, len(text))]\n\n    return codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | vice versa)\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n    if span_norm in code_norm:\n        return 0.85\n    if code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.70\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword matching score.\n    Handles both English and Chinese text by checking substrings and tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keyword_matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n\n        # Check for token overlap or substring match\n        # This handles both English and Chinese text\n        if kw_tokens & span_tokens or kw_norm in span_norm:\n            keyword_matches += 1\n\n    # Score based on proportion of matched keywords\n    score = min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n    return score\n\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component category_lv2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a score based on category match.\n    Maps categories to likely keywords and checks for presence.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n\n    # Map category patterns to keywords\n    category_patterns = {\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', 'CURRENT_HIGH': ['静态电流偏高', 'idle current', 'current high'],\n        'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'quiescent'],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', '写入失败', 'program', 'reflash', 'write failed'],\n 'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'electrostatic'],\n        'MISSING': ['missing', '缺少', '缺件', 'not found'],\n        'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', '反装'],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', '连接器', 'wear', candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Category match\n    4. Component references\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n\n    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n\n    # 2. calibrate_confidence_distribution(unknown_conf: List[float], known_conf: List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence to ensure proper statistical known_mean > 0:\n        known_scale = target_known_mean / known_mean\n        known_conf = [min(0.95, max(0.65, c * known_scale)) for c in known_conf]\n\n    return unknown_conf, known_conf\n\n\ndef \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n   190→    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n   191→    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n   192→\n 239→\n   240→\n   241→def extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n   242→    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n   243→    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n   244→
