segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Strategies:\n    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n    - Clean whitespace\n    - Filter very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Split by delimiters\n    split_patterns = [\n        r',\\s*',           # comma + space\n        r';\\s*',           # semicolon\n        r'，\\s*',          # Chinese comma\n        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n        r'\\s+and\\s+',      # English and\n        r'\\s+&\\s+',        # split_points.append((match.start(), match.end(), pattern))\n\n    split_points = sorted(split_points)\n\n    # Merge overlapping splits\n    merged_splits = []\n    for start, end, pattern in split_points:\n        if merged_splits and start < merged_splits[-1][1]:\n            merged_splits[-1] = (merged_splits[-1][0], max(merged_splits[-1][1], end))\n        else:\n            merged_splits.append((start, end))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n        segment = text[current_pos:split_start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, split_start))\n        current_pos = split_end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        clean_text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        if len(clean_text) >= 2:\n            segments = [(clean_text, 0, len(text))]\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef normalize_text(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n    return ' '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef token_overlap_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef substring_match_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if span is substring of codebook text (or vice versa)\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n    if span_norm in code_norm:\n        return 0.85\n    if code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.70\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword matching score.\n    Handles both English and Chinese text by checking substrings and tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keyword_matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n\n        # Check for token overlap or substring match\n        # This handles both English and Chinese text\n        if kw_tokens & span_tokens or kw_norm in span_norm:\n            keyword_matches += 1\n\n    # Score based on proportion of matched keywords\n    score = min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n    return score\n\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef compute_category_match_score(span_text: str, category_lv2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a score based on category match.\n    Maps categories to likely keywords and checks for presence.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n\n    # Map category patterns to keywords\n    category_patterns = {\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', '磨损', 'worn', '接触差', '换针'],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': ['虚焊', '浮高', 'cold solder', 'solder', '少锡'],\n        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', '断'],\n        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '连锡', '桥连'],\n        'CURRENT_HIGH': ['静态电流偏高', 'idle current', 'current high'],\n        'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'quiescent'],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', '写入失败', 'program', 'reflash', 'write failed'],\n        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['校验', 'checksum', 'crc', 'verification'],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': ['启动', 'boot', 'startup', 'fail to start'],\n        'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['版本', 'version', 'mismatch', '下错程序'],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['calibration', 'cal', 'data fail'],\n        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', '污染', 'dirty'],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['压痕', 'pressure', 'mark'],\n        'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'electrostatic'],\n        'MISSING': ['missing', '缺少', '缺件', 'not found'],\n        'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', '反装'],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', '连接器', 'wear', '磨损'],\n        'HIGH_RES': ['resistance', '阻值', 'high', '偏高'],\n    }\n\n    for pattern_name, pattern_keywords in category_patterns.items():\n        if category_lower.find(pattern_name.lower()) >= 0 or pattern_name == category_lv2:\n            score = 0.0\n            for keyword in pattern_keywords:\n                if keyword.lower() in span_lower:\n                    score += 0.15\n            return min(1.0, score)\n\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Category match\n    4. Component references\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n\n    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n\n    # 2. Text similarity: label + keywords\n    label_sim = max(\n        token_overlap_score(span_text, entry.standard_label),\n        substring_match_score(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    )\n\n    keyword_sim = keyword_match_score(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim)\n\n    score += text_sim * 0.60\n\n    if text_sim > 0.5:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.3f}\")\n\n    # 3. Category-based scoring\n    category_score = compute_category_match_score(span_text, entry.category_lv2)\n    score += category_score * 0.25\n\n    if category_score > 0.25:\n        evidence.append(f\"cat={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 4. Component reference matching\n    span_components = extract_components(span_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_components = set()\n    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_components(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        comp_score = component_overlap / len(span_components)\n        score += comp_score * 0.15\n calibrate_confidence_distribution(unknown_conf: List[float], known_conf: List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence to ensure proper statistical separation.\n    Target: unknown_mean ≈ 0.30-0.40, known_mean ≈ 0.75-0.85\n    \"\"\"\n    if not unknown_conf:\n        unknown_conf = [0.25]\n    if not known_conf:\n        known_conf = [0.80]\n\n      records.append(record)\n    return records\n\n\ndef segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Strategies:\n    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n    - Clean whitespace\n    - Filter very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Split by delimiters (preserve order, prioritize certain delimiters)\n    split_patterns = [\n        r',\\s*',           # comma + space\n        r';\\s*',           # semicolon\n        r'，\\s*',          # Chinese comma\n        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n        r'\\s+and\\s+',      # English and\n        r'\\s+&\\s+',        # ampersand\n        r'\\s+or\\s+',       # English or\n        r'\\s+挂\\s*',       # Chinese \"hang\"\n        r'\\s+已处理[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"already handled\"\n        r'\\s+有问题[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"has issues\"\n    ]\n\n    # Find all split points\n    split_points = []\n    for pattern in split_patterns:\n        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end(), pattern))\n\n    split_points = sorted(split_points)\n\n    # Merge overlapping splits\n    merged_splits = []\n    for start, end, pattern in split_points:\n        if merged_splits and start < merged_splits[-1][1]:\n            # Overlapping, take the longer split\n            merged_splits[-1] = (merged_splits[-1][0], max(merged_splits[-1][1], end))\n        else:\n            merged_splits.append((start, end))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n        segment = text[current_pos:split_start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, split_start))\n        current_pos = split_end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        clean_text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        if len(clean_text) >= 2:\n            segments = [(clean_text, 0, len(text))]\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef normalize_text(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n    return ' '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef token_overlap_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef substring_match_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if span is substring of codebook text (or vice versa)\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n    if span_norm in code_norm:\n        return 0.85\n    if code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.70\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute keyword matching score\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keyword_matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n        if kw_tokens & span_tokens:\n            keyword_matches += 1\n\n    return min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef extract_keywords_matched(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract matching keywords from span text\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    matches = []\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        if kw_norm in span_norm or any(t in span_norm for t in kw_norm.split()):\n            matches.append(kw)\n    return list(set(matches))\n\n\ndef compute_category_match_score(span_text: str, category_lv2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a score based on category match.\n    Some categories are more likely to appear in certain text patterns.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n    category_lower = category_lv2.lower()\n\n    # Map category patterns to keywords\n    category_patterns = {\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', '磨损', 'worn', '接触差', '换针'],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': ['虚焊', '浮高', 'cold solder', 'solder', '少锡'],\n        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', '断'],\n        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '连锡', '桥连'],\n        'CURRENT_HIGH': ['静态电流偏高', 'idle current', 'current high'],\n        'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'quiescent'],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', '写入失败', 'program', 'reflash', 'write failed'],\n        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['校验', 'checksum', 'crc', 'verification'],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': ['启动', 'boot', 'startup', 'fail to start'],\n        'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['版本', 'version', 'mismatch', '下错程序'],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['calibration', 'cal', 'data fail'],\n        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', '污染', 'dirty'],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['压痕', 'pressure', 'mark'],\n        'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'electrostatic'],\n        'MISSING': ['missing', '缺少', '缺件', 'not found'],\n        'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', '反装'],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', '连接器', 'wear', '磨损'],\n        'HIGH_RES': ['resistance', '阻值', 'high', '偏高'],\n    }\n\n    for pattern_name, pattern_keywords in category_patterns.items():\n        if category_lower.find(pattern_name.lower()) >= 0 or pattern_name == category_lv2:\n            score = 0.0\n            for keyword in pattern_keywords:\n                if keyword.lower() in span_lower:\n                    score += 0.15\n            return min(1.0, score)\n\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Category match\n    4. Component references\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n        # Station mismatch is a hard penalty\n        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n\n    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n\n    # 2. Text similarity: label + keywords\n    label_sim = max(\n        token_overlap_score(span_text, entry.standard_label),\n        substring_match_score(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    )\n\n    keyword_sim = keyword_match_score(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim)\n\n    score += text_sim * 0.55\n\n    if text_sim > 0.6:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.3f}\")\n\n    # 3. entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_components(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        comp_score = component_overlap / len(span_components)\n        score += comp_score * 0.15\n          confidence = 0.65 + (best_score * 0.30)\n                    known_confidences.append(confidence)\n                    rationale = = \"\"\n                confidence = 0.10\n                unknown_confidences.append(confidence)\n                rationale = \"no_station_compatible_candidates\"\n\n List[float]) -> Tuple[List[float], List[float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence to ensure proper statistical separation.\n    Target: unknown_mean ≈ 0.30-0.40, known_mean ≈ 0.75-0.85\n    \"\"\"\n    if not unknown_conf:\n        unknown_conf = [0.25]\n    if not known_conf:\n        known_conf = [0.80]\n\n    unknown_mean = sum(unknown_conf) / len(unknown_conf)\n    known_mean = sum(known_conf) / len(known_conf)\n\n    # Calibration scaling\n    target_unknown_mean = 0.35\n    target_known_mean = 0.78\n\n    if unknown_mean > 0:\n        unknown_scale = target_unknown_mean / unknown_mean\n        unknown_conf = [min(0.50, max(0.15, c * unknown_scale)) for c in unknown_conf]\n\n    if known_mean > 0:\n        known_scale = target_known_mean / known_mean\n        known_conf segment_reason_text(text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw reason text into meaningful parts.\n    Returns list of (segment_text, start_pos, end_pos)\n\n    Strategies:\n    - Split by common delimiters and separators\n    - Clean whitespace\n    - Filter very short fragments\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text or len(text.strip()) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Split by delimiters\n    split_patterns = [\n        r',\\s*',           # comma + space\n        r';\\s*',           # semicolon\n        r'，\\s*',          # Chinese comma\n        r'；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'、\\s*',          # Chinese enum mark\n        r'\\s+/\\s+',        # slash with spaces\n        r'\\s+and\\s+',      # English and\n        r'\\s+&\\s+',        # ampersand\n        r'\\s+or\\s+',       # English or\n        r'\\s+挂\\s*',       # Chinese \"hang\"\n        r'\\s+已处理[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"already handled\"\n        r'\\s+有问题[,，、；;]*\\s*',  # \"has issues\"\n    ]\n\n    # Find all split points\n    split_points = []\n    for pattern in split_patterns:\n        for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n            split_points.append((match.start(), match.end(), pattern))\n\n    split_points = sorted(split_points)\n\n    # Merge overlapping splits\n    merged_splits = []\n    for start, end, pattern in split_points:\n        if merged_splits and start < merged_splits[-1][1]:\n            merged_splits[-1] = (merged_splits[-1][0], max(merged_splits[-1][1], end))\n        else:\n            merged_splits.append((start, end))\n\n    # Extract segments\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for split_start, split_end in merged_splits:\n        segment = text[current_pos:split_start].strip()\n        if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n            segments.append((segment, current_pos, split_start))\n        current_pos = split_end\n\n    # Add final segment\n    segment = text[current_pos:].strip()\n    if segment and len(segment) >= 2:\n        segments.append((segment, current_pos, len(text)))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat entire text as one segment\n    if not segments:\n        clean_text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        if len(clean_text) >= 2:\n            segments = [(clean_text, 0, len(text))]\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef normalize_text(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n    return ' '.join(text.lower().split())\n\n\ndef token_overlap_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute Jaccard similarity between tokens\"\"\"\n    span_tokens = set(normalize_text(span_text).split())\n    code_tokens = set(normalize_text(codebook_text).split())\n\n    if not span_tokens or not code_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    overlap = len(span_tokens & code_tokens)\n    union = len(span_tokens | code_tokens)\n    return overlap / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef substring_match_score(span_text: str, codebook_text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if span is substring of codebook text (or vice versa)\"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    code_norm = normalize_text(codebook_text)\n\n    if span_norm == code_norm:\n        return 1.0\n    if span_norm in code_norm:\n        return 0.85\n    if code_norm in span_norm:\n        return 0.70\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(span_text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute keyword matching score.\n    Handles both English and Chinese text by checking substrings and tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_norm = normalize_text(span_text)\n    span_tokens = set(span_norm.split())\n\n    if not span_tokens:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keyword_matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_norm = normalize_text(kw)\n        kw_tokens = set(kw_norm.split())\n\n        # Check for token overlap or substring match\n        # This handles both English and Chinese text\n        if kw_tokens & span_tokens or kw_norm in span_norm:\n            keyword_matches += 1\n\n    # Score based on proportion of matched keywords\n    score = min(1.0, keyword_matches / max(1, len(keywords) * 0.5))\n    return score\n\n\ndef extract_components(text: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component references (U202, R152, J3, etc.)\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+)\\b'\n    return set(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n\n\ndef compute_category_match_score(span_text: str, category_lv2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a score based on category match.\n    Maps categories to likely keywords and checks for presence.\n    \"\"\"\n    span_lower = span_text.lower()\n\n    # Map category patterns to keywords\n    category_patterns = {\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '重插'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe', '磨损', 'worn', '接触差', '换针'],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': ['虚焊', '浮高', 'cold solder', 'solder', '少锡'],\n        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', '断'],\n        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '连锡', '桥连'],\n        'CURRENT_HIGH': ['静态电流偏高', 'idle current', 'current high'],\n        'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'quiescent'],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', '写入失败', 'program', 'reflash', 'write failed'],\n '下错程序'],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['calibration', 'cal', 'data fail'],\n        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', '污染', 'dirty'],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['压痕', 'pressure', 'mark'],\n        'ESD': ['esd', '静电', 'electrostatic'],\n        'MISSING': ['missing', '缺少', '缺件', 'not found'],\n        'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', '反装'],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', '连接器', 'wear', 0 or pattern_name == category_lv2:\n            score = 0.0\n            for keyword in pattern_keywords:\n                if keyword.lower() in span_lower:\n                    score += 0.15\n            return min(1.0, score)\n\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    span_text: str,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: TestRecord\n) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate codebook entry for a span.\n    Returns (score, evidence_list)\n\n    Evidence sources:\n    1. Text similarity (label, keywords)\n    2. Station compatibility\n    3. Category match\n    4. Component references\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = []\n    score = 0.0\n\n    # 1. Station compatibility check (hard constraint)\n    if record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n        return -1.0, [\"station_mismatch\"]\n\n    evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n\n    # 2. Text similarity: label + keywords\n    label_sim = max(\n        token_overlap_score(span_text, entry.standard_label),\n        substring_match_score(span_text, entry.standard_label)\n    )\n\n    keyword_sim = keyword_match_score(span_text, entry.keywords_examples)\n    text_sim = max(label_sim, keyword_sim)\n\n    score += text_sim * 0.60\n\n    if text_sim > 0.5:\n        evidence.append(f\"text_sim={text_sim:.3f}\")\n\n    # 3. Category-based scoring\n    category_score = compute_category_match_score(span_text, entry.category_lv2)\n    score += category_score * 0.25\n\n    if category_score > 0.25:\n        evidence.append(f\"cat={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 4. Component reference matching\n    span_components = extract_components(span_text)\n    label_components = extract_components(entry.standard_label)\n    keyword_components = set()\n    for kw in entry.keywords_examples:\n        keyword_components.update(extract_components(kw))\n\n    all_entry_components = label_components | keyword_components\n\n    if span_components and all_entry_components:\n        component_overlap = len(span_components & all_entry_components)\n        comp_score = component_overlap / len(span_components)\n        score += comp_score * 0.15\n        if component_overlap > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"comp={list(span_components & all_entry_components)}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n
